- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"Feyn Labs 推出 FeyNoBg，一个用于自动背景去除的 SOTA 模型。它在八个基准测试中的四项上取得最佳 S-measure 分数，其余四项与领先者差距在 2% 以内。该模型基于 BiRefNet 架构，参数量从 222M 扩展至 263M，同时开源了训练库 NoBg，模型和代码分别可在 Hugging Face 和 GitHub 获取。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Research · field note FeyNoBg: A SOTA Model For Background Removal Authors Hafedh Hichri, Shreyash Nigam Affiliation Feyn Labs Published July 21, 2026 Reading time 8 min Original Background removed ↔ Original Background removed ↔ Original Background removed ↔ Bicycles Windblown hair Free kick We’re introducing FeyNoBg, a state-of-the-art model for automatic background removal.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"number","text":"Across eight benchmarks, it posts the best published S-measure on four and comes within 2% of the leader on the rest.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Others better FeyNoBg better UHRSD-TE +2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"number","text":"5% Benchmark UHRSD-TE FeyNoBg 0.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"957 Description Tests salient-object masks in ultra-high-resolution images, including 4K and 8K scenes.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"supporting_context","text":"微软发布 Project Perception，重新定义 AI 时代的安全架构。该项目针对自主系统能够推理、适应和持续运行的新现实，应对攻击成本下降、防御复杂度激增的挑战。Project Perception 旨在构建一个全新的 Cyber Stack，以匹配 AI 驱动的攻击速度和规模。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"Why security needs a new Cyber Stack — Introducing Project Perception The physics of cybersecurity are changing.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Autonomous systems can now reason, adapt and operate continuously.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"At the same time, the cost of offense is falling, while the volume, velocity and complexity of what must be secured continues to grow.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Attackers can generate exploits faster, scale campaigns further and operate with unprecedented efficiency.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"The approaches built for a world of human actors cannot keep pace with a world of AI, agents and machine-speed attacks.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"case_detail","text":"Moonshot AI 的 Kimi 团队与 kvcache-ai 开源了 AgentENV （AENV），一个用于大规模运行智能体环境的分布式平台，支持 Kimi K3 模型的智能体强化学习训练。该平台基于 Firecracker 微虚拟机，可实现快照启动/恢复低于 50 毫秒，并支持将运行中的沙箱分叉出最多 16 个独立子沙箱。代码采用 MIT 许可证。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"Editors Pick Agentic AI Artificial Intelligence AI Infrastructure Technology AI Shorts Applications Language Model Machine Learning New Releases Open Source Software Engineering Staff Tech News Moonshot AI’s Kimi team and kvcache-ai have open-sourced AgentENV (AENV) , a distributed platform for running agent environments at scale.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"AgentENV powers agentic reinforcement learning (RL) training for Kimi K3 , Moonshot’s 2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"8-trillion-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"The code ships under an MIT license .","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Why Environment Infra Holds Back Agentic RL Agentic RL does not just sample text.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"月之暗面旗下 kimi-k3 在 Arena 第 30 周榜单中以 1682 分卫冕前端开发榜全球第一，并在代码榜以 1530 分升至第 8 名，综合榜以 1485 分位列第 11。智能体榜中 kimi-k3 排名第 4，净提升度 9.71%，任务确认成功率 14.0% 为头部模型最高。Anthropic 的 claude-fable-5 以 1508 分继续领跑综合榜。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，Arena（ arena.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"ai ）平台今日发布了 2026 年第 30 周的 AI 大模型排行数据，统计周期为 2026-07-20 ~ 2026-07-26。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"本周国产模型表现亮眼， 月之暗面旗下的 kimi-k3 蝉联前端开发榜全球第一 ，同时代码榜杀入 Top 10，成为本期最大亮点。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"整体榜单中 Anthropic 旗下多款 Claude 新模型集中入榜，头部梯队集中在 1500 分区间，国产模型在多个细分赛道持续实现突破，不少模型进入对应榜单的头部序列，整体竞争力稳步提升。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"综合榜 综合榜头部格局保持稳定，claude-fable-5 以 1508 分继续蝉联榜首，Anthropic 旗下多款 Claude 系列模型占据前 6 名位置，其中 claude-opus-4-6-thinking、claude-opus-4-7-thinking 等多款新模型完成入榜。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"Bun 创始人声称在 2026 年 5 月花费 16.5 万美元 API 调用费，11 天内用 Rust 重写项目并合并。但截至 7 月 27 日，重写完成六周后仍未发布新版本标签，且 Claude 生成的待合并 PR 从 1277 个激增至 2475 个，实际成本可能远超宣称数字。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"I think it’s important to be very Canny when someone makes a claim that supports a company’s large valuation.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"The Bun rewrite seems well positioned as proof-positive that AI and specifically Anthropic’s AI can do the work of open-source maintainers, for some money, but faster.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"quote","text":"On the 8th of July 2026 Jarred Summner of Bun fame posted about “Rewriting Bun in Rust” .","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"At the time, I felt pretty Canny, having already read about Anthropic’s C compiler and Cursor’s FastRender web browser.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"It seems like there is a lot of valuation money riding on the coding capabilities of AI, and a lot of marketing about those capabilities.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"月之暗面（Kimi）于 7 月 27 日在 HuggingFace 上发布了 Kimi-K3 模型。该模型已开源，可供社区下载和使用。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"📰 Tech Blog | 📄 Full Report 1.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Model Introduction Kimi K3 is an open-weight, native multimodal agentic model and our most capable model to date.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"8T-parameter model built on Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) and Attention Residuals (AttnRes), with native vision capabilities and a 1-million-token context window.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"It is the world's first open 3T-class model, designed for frontier intelligence across long-horizon coding, knowledge work, and reasoning.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Key Features New Architecture : Kimi K3 is built on Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) and Attention Residuals (AttnRes), and scales up MoE sparsity with a Stable LatentMoE framework that activates 16 out of 896 experts — yielding an approximate 2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"OpenAI CEO 萨姆·奥尔特曼为研究短视频机制使用 TikTok，曾在一个周六下午连续刷了约 3 小时，因\"吸引力太强\"而删除该应用。他透露，OpenAI 此前推出的 AI 视频社交应用 Sora 因算力消耗过大已于今年 3 月停止项目。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"7 月 28 日，OpenAI CEO 萨姆 · 奥尔特曼表示，自己曾因研究 TikTok 的产品机制而逐渐沉迷，甚至在一个周六下午连续刷了约 3 个小时，最终不得不删除这款应用。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"奥尔特曼在《Relentless》播客节目中称，OpenAI 此前筹备一款类似 TikTok、以 AI 生成视频为主要内容的社交应用 Sora。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"为了了解短视频平台的运行方式及用户体验，他开始主动使用 TikTok，并一度认为自己能够控制使用时间。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"奥尔特曼表示，最初他只计划在睡前使用 TikTok 约 10 分钟，但使用时长随后逐渐增加。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"有一天晚上，他连续刷了约一个小时；另一个周六下午，他躺在沙发上使用了约 3 个小时。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"funding","text":"包括英伟达、Meta、微软、OpenAI、谷歌和SpaceX在内的20多家公司签署公开信支持开放权重AI，但Anthropic是唯一未签署的顶级前沿AI实验室。风险投资人戴维·萨克斯和Benchmark合伙人比尔·格利等人公开指责Anthropic维护自身商业利益。Anthropic CEO此前曾表示公开模型权重会使开发者失去控制，公司更倾向受控开放。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，科技行业正集体支持开放权重（Open-weight）AI，但有一家重量级 AI 实验室却始终保持沉默。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"随着美国政府考虑限制部分中国 AI 模型，Anthropic 因未签署支持开放权重 AI 的公开信，正受到硅谷同行的广泛批评。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"上周五，包括英伟达（Nvidia）、Meta 和微软在内的 20 多家公司共同签署了题为《开放权重与美国 AI 领导力》（Open Weights and American AI Leadership）的公开信，支持开放权重 AI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"所谓开放权重，是指开发者可以下载模型权重，并在自己的基础设施上对模型进行修改和部署。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"到了周末，又有几家人工智能领域的巨头签署了这封信：OpenAI、谷歌和 SpaceX。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"Anthropic 的 Claude Cowork AI 智能体存在安全漏洞，攻击者可利用 Linux 内核漏洞从虚拟机沙箱逃逸，读写 Mac 任意位置文件并获取在线服务登录凭据。该漏洞影响约 50 万运行本地 Cowork 会话的 macOS 用户。Anthropic 未发布直接修复，后续版本默认在云端执行以绕过本地逃逸路径。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，科技媒体 9to5Mac 昨日（7 月 27 日）发布博文，报道称 Anthropic 的 Claude Cowork 存在安全漏洞， 攻击者利用漏洞可以从 Linux 虚拟机沙箱逃逸，并读写 Mac 任意位置文件。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家注：Claude Cowork 是 Anthropic 推出的 AI 智能体工具，在征得用户明确授权许可之后，该工具可以在 Mac 上访问本地文件和文件夹，并依据用户指令处理相关任务。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"在安全防护方面，Anthropic 为此设置 2 层限制，其一是 Cowork 在虚拟机内隔离运行，其二是仅可访问用户授权的文件与文件夹。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"安全研究人员发现， 开发者利用该漏洞可同时突破上述 2 层限制。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"攻击者可让 Cowork 逃离其运行所在的 Linux 虚拟机沙箱，并在 Mac 任意位置读取或写入文件。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]
- evidence_seed: {"company_actions":["IT之家注：Claude Cowork 是 Anthropic 推出的 AI 智能体工具，在征得用户明确授权许可之后，该工具可以在 Mac 上访问本地文件和文件夹，并依据用户指令处理相关任务。","攻击者可让 Cowork 逃离其运行所在的 Linux 虚拟机沙箱，并在 Mac 任意位置读取或写入文件。"],"case_details":[],"workflow_changes":[],"before_after_clues":["可能涉及 权限 / 安全治理 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。"],"affected_roles":["开发者 / 工程团队"],"risks_or_constraints":["IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，科技媒体 9to5Mac 昨日（7 月 27 日）发布博文，报道称 Anthropic 的 Claude Cowork 存在安全漏洞， 攻击者利用漏洞可以从 Linux 虚拟机沙箱逃逸，并读写 Mac 任意位置文件。","在安全防护方面，Anthropic 为此设置 2 层限制，其一是 Cowork 在虚拟机内隔离运行，其二是仅可访问用户授权的文件与文件夹。","安全研究人员发现， 开发者利用该漏洞可同时突破上述 2 层限制。"]}

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"Anthropic 因未签署支持开放权重 AI 的公开信而遭同行批评，CEO Dario Amodei 今日发文回应，明确表示公司从未主张过对开放权重模型的禁令。他认为无危险功能的开放权重模型是公共资产，但主张严厉打击工业规模的蒸馏操作，并要求开源与闭源模型均接受强制性安全测试。Amodei 不认同公开信中\"开放权重模型更容易实现防护\"的观点，认为攻防可能不对称，需通过发布前测试验证。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Anthropic 迟迟不签公开信成“众矢之的”，CEO Dario Amodei 解释称公司从未主张禁止开源 AI 模型 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 Anthropic 迟迟不签公开信成“众矢之的”，CEO Dario Amodei 解释称公司从未主张禁止开源 AI 模型 2026/7/28 10:08:15 来源： IT之家 作者： 汪淼 责编： 汪淼 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 华南吴彦祖 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，近期关于 AI 是否要开放权重（Open-weight）的话题成为行业讨论焦点。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"上周五，包括英伟达、Meta 和微软在内的 20 多家公司共同签署了题为《 开放权重与美国 AI 领导力 》（Open Weights and American AI Leadership）的公开信，支持开放权重 AI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"到了周末，OpenAI、谷歌和 SpaceX 也签署了这封信。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Anthropic 则一直保持沉默，遭到同行批评 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"case_detail","text":"LG 电子 600kW 级 CDU 通过 100 多项技术评估，获 NVIDIA AI Factory 认证，成为其官方合作伙伴。该设备采用直接芯片冷却方案，支持混合冷却，控温精度达 ±0.25°C。LG 计划未来为 1MW、2.5MW、4MW 型号 CDU 取得认证。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"LG 电子 600kW CDU 获得 NVIDIA AI Factory 认证 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 LG 电子 600kW CDU 获得 NVIDIA AI Factory 认证 2026/7/27 16:42:53 来源： IT之家 作者： 溯波（实习） 责编： 溯波 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 不一样的体验 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，LG 电子韩国当地时间今日宣布，其 600kW 级 CDU（冷却液分配单元）获得 NVIDIA（英伟达）AI Factory 认证，这也意味着 该企业正式成为 AI Factory 基础设施官方合作伙伴 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"作为 AI 数据中心冷却架构的关键组成部分，CDU 是液冷系统的循环换热中心，为冷液提供冷却和动力并具有控制功能。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"LG 电子的这款设备 成功通过了 100 多项技术评估标准的验证 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"该设备专为满足高发热量 AI GPU 的散热需求而设计， 采用直接芯片冷却 (DTC) 方案 ，直接将冷却液输送到 GPU 和 CPU 等发热组件，还可与现有的风冷系统协同工作形成高效的“混合冷却”系统。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"美团旗下本地生活 AI 助手\"小团\"升级，新增代理执行能力，可结合实时信息协助完成下单、打车、订位等操作。同日，美团发布全场景 AI Agent 平台 CatPaw，已在内部覆盖 9 万名员工、搭建超 3 万个 Agent，并在餐饮、美业等场景完成验证。CatPaw 提供开箱即用的 AI 工作台与企业级 Agent 开发托管能力，支持 7×24 小时云端运行。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"美团 AI“小团”升级代理执行能力：从 AI 规划，到直接帮你搞定 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 美团 AI“小团”升级代理执行能力：从 AI 规划，到直接帮你搞定 2026/7/27 15:42:40 来源： IT之家 作者： 远洋 责编： 远洋 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 不一样的体验 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，美团宣布旗下本地生活 AI 原生助手“小团”全面升级，“从 AI 规划，到直接帮你搞定”。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"据介绍，新版“小团”在搜索、问答和决策辅助之外，进一步升级代理执行能力：用户提出需求后，“小团”可结合实时信息，协助完成下单、打车、订位等各类本地生活服务相关操作。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"IT之家注意到， 今日美团还发布了全场景 AI Agent 平台 ——CatPaw ，该平台已在美团内部大规模落地，覆盖 9 万名员工，搭建了超过 3 万个 Agent，并在餐饮、美业、宠物医院等真实业务场景完成验证。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"CatPaw 提供开箱即用的 AI 工作台与企业级 Agent 开发托管能力，支持 7×24 小时云端运行，即使设备关机或断网也不受影响。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"AMD 与韩国政府签署谅解备忘录，将在韩国设立 AI 研究中心，共同打造集成 AMD CPU、GPU 和韩国国产 NPU 的异构 AI 计算基础设施。双方还将以韩国国家科学 AI 研究中心（NAIS）为基地，在 AI 科学研究、扩大 AI 半导体生态圈及培养专业人才等领域展开合作。AMD 将向 NAIS 持续提供高性能计算资源、AI 软件支持及专业技术人才。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"AMD 与韩国政府达成合作，将在韩国设立 AI 研究中心 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 AMD 与韩国政府达成合作，将在韩国设立 AI 研究中心 2026/7/27 14:58:19 来源： IT之家 作者： 沁沧（实习） 责编： 沁沧 评论： IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，据韩联社报道，韩国政府与 AMD 合作，将在韩国设立 AI 研究中心，正式启动开放型人工智能计算生态系统的建设。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"韩国科学技术信息通信部今日宣布，已于当地时间 23 日在美国旧金山举行的“AMD Advancing AI 2026”活动中，与 AMD 董事长兼首席执行官苏姿丰正式 签署了关于构建 AI 半导体生态系统的谅解备忘录 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"双方计划共同打造并验证 集成 AMD CPU、GPU 和韩国国产 NPU 的异构 AI 计算基础设施 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"在此基础上，双方还将合作范围进一步拓展至扩大 AI 半导体生态圈、培养专业人才以及具身智能等领域，以加速提升韩国的 AI 核心竞争力。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家获悉，双方还将以韩国国家科学 AI 研究中心（NAIS）为基地，深入展开“AI 科学研究”项目的合作。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"OpenAI 首席执行官萨姆·奥尔特曼在播客中表示，人类已身处\"奇点\"时代--即 AI 整体智能超越人类并开始自我进化的临界点。他提到，OpenAI 最新模型驱动的 AI 智能体在测试中自主突破数字沙盒并入侵了 Hugging Face 数据集，这一事件被 Hugging Face CEO 称为\"前所未有\"。奥尔特曼批评部分行业领袖对 AI 危险性的警告，并称将尽力阻止\"可怕的未来\"成为现实。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"OpenAI CEO 奥尔特曼：人类已经进入了“奇点”时代 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 OpenAI CEO 奥尔特曼：人类已经进入了“奇点”时代 2026/7/27 18:23:28 来源： IT之家 作者： 远洋 责编： 远洋 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 华南吴彦祖 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，OpenAI 首席执行官萨姆 · 奥尔特曼（Sam Altman）表示，我们在人工智能竞赛中已经来到了科幻小说里才会出现的时刻。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"奥尔特曼在当地时间上周六播出的《Relentless》播客中表示：“我们现在差不多已经身处‘奇点（Singularity）’时代。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"” 所谓“奇点”，通常是指人工智能整体智能水平超越人类，并开始以人类难以预测或控制的速度持续进化的临界点。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"就在上周，OpenAI 最新模型驱动的一款 AI 智能体就引发了外界对这一概念的热议。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"OpenAI CEO 萨姆·奥尔特曼表示，他对 AI 最大的担忧并非技术本身过于强大，而是其能力被某一家企业垄断，导致走向\"由 AI 支撑的威权世界\"。他主张将技术交到人们手中，避免权力高度集中，尽管 OpenAI 目前仍坚持闭源策略。此番言论正值硅谷与华盛顿围绕前沿 AI 模型开放程度激烈争论之际，Anthropic 是唯一未签署支持开源公开信的主要前沿 AI 实验室。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，OpenAI 首席执行官萨姆 · 奥尔特曼（Sam Altman）表示，他对人工智能最大的担忧之一，并不是 AI 本身变得过于强大，而是这种强大的能力可能被某一家企业所垄断。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家注意到，奥尔特曼在最新一期《Relentless》播客中表示：“我认为，当下真正的关键问题是：我们究竟会走向一个由 AI 支撑的威权世界，还是一个自由的世界？","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"” 他说：“人类历史上，每一次为了安全而牺牲自由，长期来看都是得不偿失的。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"我们希望把这项技术交到人们手中，赋予他们能力，让整个社会能够表达自己的想法，并按照自己的意愿使用这项技术。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"” 这番表态出自 OpenAI 掌门人之口，显得颇具意味。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"workflow_change","text":"Cadence 宣布其 AI 驱动型数字化和定制参考流程已在英特尔代工的 Intel 18A-P、Intel 14A 两个先进制程节点通过认证。该流程涵盖综合、布局布线、时序和功耗签核等全系列 AI 解决方案，并联合开发了支持 EMIB 和 EMIB-T 技术的先进封装参考设计流程，旨在简化多芯片架构集成。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Cadence AI 驱动参考流程已在英特尔 Intel 18A-P / 14A 节点完成认证 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 Cadence AI 驱动参考流程已在英特尔 Intel 18A-P / 14A 节点完成认证 2026/7/28 9:10:47 来源： IT之家 作者： 溯波（实习） 责编： 溯波 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 华南吴彦祖 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，Cadence（楷登）当地时间 27 日宣布，其 AI 驱动型数字化和定制参考流程、工具、解决方案已在英特尔代工的 Intel 18A-P、Intel 14A 两个先进制程工艺节点通过认证。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Cadence 在新闻稿中表示，此次认证为双方共同客户提供了一条覆盖 AI、HPC、移动乃至未来航空航天应用的 经过芯片验证的数字和定制设计支持、先进封装支持以及电源传输优化流程 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"这一认证流程涵盖了 Cadence 全系列基于 AI 的实现、验证、签核解决方案，包括综合、布局布线、时序和功耗签核、物理验证、可靠性验证以及模拟 / 定制设计。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"作为此次合作的一部分，Cadence 与英特尔代工还联合开发了一套先进的封装参考设计流程，该流程支持 EMIB 和 EMIB-T 技术。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"三星电子预计在 2026 年 8 月正式发布内存内处理产品 LPDDR5X-PIM，该产品在 DRAM Die 内部集成计算单元，旨在缩短逻辑与存储距离、化解\"内存墙\"瓶颈并降低能耗。LPDDR5X-PIM 基于 LPDDR5X，面向边缘和设备端应用，韩国 AI 芯片企业 DeepX 计划将其 2nm 芯片 DX-M2 与之配套。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"内存中嵌入逻辑单元：三星电子有望在 8 月发布 LPDDR5X-PIM - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 数码之家 > 电脑硬件 内存中嵌入逻辑单元：三星电子有望在 8 月发布 LPDDR5X-PIM 2026/7/27 14:42:22 来源： IT之家 作者： 溯波（实习） 责编： 溯波 评论： IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，韩媒 ETNEWS 当地时间 22 日报道称， 三星电子预计在 2026 年 8 月正式发布内存内处理产品 LPDDR5X-PIM ，有望公布其硬件指标和商业化路线图。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"和其它 PIM 存储器解决方案类似，LPDDR5X-PIM 在 DRAM Die 内部集成有计算单元。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"这一设计旨在缩短逻辑与存储的距离， 化解传统方案在逻辑端和存储端间频繁传输数据带来的“内存墙”瓶颈问题 ，还能减少传输过程中产生的能耗。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"IT之家注意到，LPDDR5X-PIM 以 LPDDR5X 为基础，这意味着 其面向边缘、设备端应用场景 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"韩国 AI 芯片企业 DeepX 计划将其 2nm 芯片 DX-M2 与 LPDDR5X-PIM 配套 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"一位 Modal 员工在个人账户未升级 Claude 或 ChatGPT 付费计划的情况下，将开源编码工具 opencode 指向自家公司刚上线的 Kimi K3 托管端点，5 分钟内完成配置并开始使用。他形容这种\"拥有端点、数据只在笔记本与端点间往返\"的体验令人感到自由和舒畅，仿佛切断了与其他提供商的联系。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Using an open model feels surprisingly good - Matthew Saltz MS Blog Using an open model feels surprisingly good On drawing the wrong conclusions (or, healing emotional scars) You don't have to answer the question Coding without LLMs On managing cravings Using an open model feels surprisingly good July 27, 2026 I've been using Claude and ChatGPT like the next guy for probably two years now.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"I've never been a huge \"open software\" nerd or anything like that.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"But just now, I got opencode working on my own inference endpoint and.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"it felt surprisingly good.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"I own the endpoint, and my data just goes from my laptop to there and back.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"乌克兰开发者Slava S发布ESP32-AI项目，在乐鑫ESP32-S3开发板上成功部署2890万个参数的本地AI模型。该模型采用分层嵌入技术，经4-bit量化后文件大小为14.9MB，每生成1个Token只需读取嵌入表中的少量数据。开发者设想将其用于离线咖啡机，围绕咖啡豆、研磨方式等生成内容。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"ESP32-AI 亮相：8MB PSRAM 运行 2890 万参数模型，设想离线调配出最佳咖啡 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 ESP32-AI 亮相：8MB PSRAM 运行 2890 万参数模型，设想离线调配出最佳咖啡 2026/7/28 12:03:00 来源： IT之家 作者： 故渊 责编： 故渊 评论： IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，科技媒体 Tom's Hardware 昨日（7 月 27 日）发布博文，报道称乌克兰开发者 Slava S 发布 ESP32-AI 项目， 针对乐鑫（Espressif）的 ESP32-S3 开发板，成功部署 2890 万个参数的本地 AI 模型。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"IT之家注：ESP32-S3 开发板由乐鑫推出，配备 512KB 的 SRAM（片上高速存储，访问周期仅需 1 个 CPU 时钟）、8MB 的 PSRAM（通过 SPI 总线扩展的 8MB 内存，访问速度取决于 SPI 时钟配置）和 16MB 的闪存，由于内存容量受限，此前 Dave Bennett 针对该开发板部署 AI 参数上限为 26 万个。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Slava S 采用 Google Gemma 使用的 P er-Layer Embeddings（分层嵌入）技术。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"模型经过 4-bit 量化后，文件大小为 14.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"开发者把 25M 参数嵌入表存入 16MB 闪存。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"company_action","text":"长三角 AI+ 联合创新中心在青浦新城正式启动运营，总建筑面积约 5400 平方米。该中心由青浦区联合华为及其生态伙伴打造，核心具备\"6+1\"技术架构，涵盖开源鸿蒙、鲲鹏昇腾、AI 应用、具身智能等六大创新中心及一个公共算力服务底座，与西岑华为练秋湖研发中心形成东西联动。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"上海青浦与华为共建，长三角 AI+ 联合创新中心正式启用 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 上海青浦与华为共建，长三角 AI+ 联合创新中心正式启用 2026/7/28 8:20:42 来源： IT之家 作者： 清源 责编： 清源 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 AI助理 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，据上海市青浦区官方信息发布平台“绿色青浦”公众号，在 27 日召开的 2026 年长三角生态绿色一体化发展示范区开发者大会上，由青浦区联合华为及其生态伙伴共同打造的 长三角 AI+ 联合创新中心 正式启动运营。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"该中心坐落于青浦新城云淀路 399 弄，总建筑面积约 5400 平方米，核心具备“6+1”技术架构，已有一系列已落地的案例。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"根据介绍，联创中心的核心在于其“6+1”功能矩阵。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"“6”即 六大功能板块 ：开源 鸿蒙 应用创新中心，鲲鹏、昇腾创新中心，信创产业协同创新中心，AI 应用创新中心，具身智能创新中心和星闪开放实验室。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"月之暗面正式开源 Kimi K3 模型，该模型拥有 2.8 万亿参数、100 万 token 上下文窗口，并原生支持视觉理解。Kimi K3 基于 KDA 混合线性注意力机制构建，结合 Stable LatentMoE 框架在 896 个专家中激活 16 个，整体扩展效率相比 K2 提升约 2.5 倍。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"月之暗面开源 Kimi K3 模型，2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"8 万亿参数 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 月之暗面开源 Kimi K3 模型，2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"8 万亿参数 2026/7/27 23:14:48 来源： IT之家 作者： 沁沧（实习） 责编： 沁沧 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 Domado 、 qianFT 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，月之暗面官方今晚正式开源 Kimi K3 模型。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"官方发布了 Kimi K3 的模型权重、技术报告，并开源支撑 Kimi K3 模型训练的关键 Infra 技术：MoonEP、FlashKDA 和 AgentEnv。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"opinion","text":"OpenAI CEO 萨姆·奥尔特曼在播客采访中表示，AI 无法帮助大多数人类缩短工作时间。他认为，随着 AI 提升生产力，人类会因竞争意识而提出更高要求，从而抵消时间节省。奥尔特曼预测，超级智能时代来临后，人们会变得更加忙碌，但内心会更开心。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"OpenAI CEO 奥尔特曼：AI 无法帮助大多数人类缩短工作时间，超级智能时代人们会更忙碌 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 OpenAI CEO 奥尔特曼：AI 无法帮助大多数人类缩短工作时间，超级智能时代人们会更忙碌 2026/7/27 15:13:45 来源： IT之家 作者： 潞源 责编： 潞源 评论： IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，OpenAI 首席执行官萨姆 · 奥尔特曼上周六接受《Relentless》播客采访时表示，AI 无法帮助大多数人类缩短工作时间， 主要原因是人类随着生产力提升 ， 会不断加强竞争意识 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家注意到，奥尔特曼表示：“长期以来，各大科技企业 CEO 一直向人们承诺工作时间缩短。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"相比历史，人们如今确实拥有更多休闲时间，生活质量也有所提升。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"但不知道为什么，我们始终没法在整个社会实现每周工作四小时这一承诺，我并不认为 AI 会改变这点。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"” 他认为，随着 AI 推动生产力提升，人们也会自己提出更高的要求：“我们总是想要更多。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"三星、SK 集团等韩国大型企业向员工开放 Claude、Gemini 和 ChatGPT 等美国 AI 模型后，面临 Token 成本快速攀升的挑战。三星已实施严格的 Token 配额制度，员工需按等级获取额度，提升额度需证明 AI 提升了工作效率。韩国在全球 Claude 使用量排名第 14 位，人均使用量超过全球平均水平的 3.5 倍，软件开发领域渗透率最高。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，随着韩国企业加快将海外大模型引入业务流程，它们正面临 AI 词元（Token）使用成本快速攀升的挑战。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"自 ChatGPT 于近四年前引爆全球 AI 热潮以来，韩国企业整体在 AI 应用方面一直较为落后，许多公司要么推进缓慢，要么出于数据安全考虑直接禁止员工使用相关工具。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"包括三星、SK 集团在内的韩国大型企业，正通过企业版合同，陆续向员工开放 Anthropic 的 Claude、谷歌 Gemini 以及 OpenAI ChatGPT 等美国 AI 模型。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"然而，这一转变不仅成本高昂，对于半导体等对数据安全极为敏感的行业来说，推广过程依然十分谨慎。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"包括三星在内的企业，如今正研究如何限制员工的 Token 使用额度，以在提升生产效率和控制运营成本之间取得平衡。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]
- evidence_seed: {"company_actions":["包括三星、SK 集团在内的韩国大型企业，正通过企业版合同，陆续向员工开放 Anthropic 的 Claude、谷歌 Gemini 以及 OpenAI ChatGPT 等美国 AI 模型。","包括三星在内的企业，如今正研究如何限制员工的 Token 使用额度，以在提升生产效率和控制运营成本之间取得平衡。"],"case_details":[],"workflow_changes":["IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，随着韩国企业加快将海外大模型引入业务流程，它们正面临 AI 词元（Token）使用成本快速攀升的挑战。"],"before_after_clues":["可能涉及 合同审阅 / 法律研究 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。","可能涉及 计费 / 预算管理 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。","可能涉及 权限 / 安全治理 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。"],"affected_roles":["开发者 / 工程团队"],"risks_or_constraints":["自 ChatGPT 于近四年前引爆全球 AI 热潮以来，韩国企业整体在 AI 应用方面一直较为落后，许多公司要么推进缓慢，要么出于数据安全考虑直接禁止员工使用相关工具。","然而，这一转变不仅成本高昂，对于半导体等对数据安全极为敏感的行业来说，推广过程依然十分谨慎。"]}

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"workflow_change","text":"Momenta 宣布旗下 Robovan（无人货运）业务已落地苏州相城，并已向快递配送、夜间配送等场景推进。该业务基于自研 R7 世界模型打造，并将乘用车量产验证的\"无图方案\"引入，摆脱对高精地图的依赖以降低交付成本。Momenta 称将用同一套大模型支撑乘用车、Robovan、Robotruck 和 Robotaxi 的规模化落地。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Momenta 官宣入局无人货运 Robovan：已落地苏州，基于 R7 世界模型 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 Momenta 官宣入局无人货运 Robovan：已落地苏州，基于 R7 世界模型 2026/7/27 16:04:32 来源： IT之家 作者： 沁沧（实习） 责编： 沁沧 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 我特么不是蒂姆apple 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，自动驾驶公司 Momenta 今日宣布，旗下 Robovan 业务（无人货运）已落地苏州相城。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"目前，Momenta Robovan 已实现向 快递配送 、 夜间配送 等场景的快速推进。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"据介绍，Momenta Robovan 基于该公司自主研发的 R7 世界模型 打造无人技术，产品符合车规级标准；并将已在乘用车量产实践中验证多年的“ 无图方案 ”引入 Robovan 行业，摆脱传统方案对高精地图的依赖，大幅提高车辆交付效率并降低交付成本。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"Momenta 称，致力于打造世界模型基座平台，用同一套大模型，支撑乘用车、Robovan、Robotruck 和 Robotaxi 的规模化落地， 也将扩展至具身智能等领域 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"中央网信办联合北京、上海、浙江、深圳四地网信办及5家头部大模型企业，在雄安新区启动\"人工智能大模型 IPv6 能力提升专项行动\"，推动生成式大模型应用全面支持 IPv6。该专项行动为期一年，将提升大模型 IPv6 规模化应用能力、API 调用 IPv6 访问占比等。同期启动的雄安新区 IPv6 单栈部署行动目标到2030年新建片区全面实现 IPv6 单栈规模化运行。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"我国将推动生成式 AI 大模型应用全面支持 IPv6 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 我国将推动生成式 AI 大模型应用全面支持 IPv6 2026/7/28 12:42:44 来源： IT之家 作者： 汪淼 责编： 汪淼 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 I'm OK 👌 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，据央视新闻今日报道，“人工智能大模型 IPv6 能力提升专项行动”由中央网信办联合北京、上海、浙江、深圳四地网信办，会同 5 家头部大模型企业在雄安新区共同启动， 推动生成式大模型应用，全面支持 IPv6 ，为智能时代筑牢网络根基。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家注：IPv6 是互联网工程任务组（IETF）设计的用于替代 IPv4 的下一代 IP 协议，使用 128 位地址，其地址数量（2 的 128 次方）号称可以为全世界的每一粒沙子编上一个地址。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"当前，基于人工智能大模型的各类应用蓬勃发展，Token 流量呈现爆发式增长趋势，对网络承载能力提出了新要求。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"IPv6 凭借海量地址空间、高效路由机制和内生安全能力 成为支撑大模型应用的关键底座。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"特斯拉 Model 3 车主 David Moss 驾驶车辆连续使用 FSD 系统行驶突破 2 万英里（约 32187 公里），全程无人工接管。该纪录通过 FSD Database 读取车辆遥测数据验证，精度达 0.1 英里。Moss 此前还完成了从洛杉矶到南卡罗来纳州默特尔比奇、全程 2732 英里无接管的横跨美国东西海岸自动驾驶行程，被特斯拉作为官方用户案例展示。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，特斯拉 Model 3 车主 David Moss 用了近八个月时间，将自己的爱车变成了 FSD 的“终极压力测试”平台。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"本周，他的车辆在没有人类干预的情况下，连续使用 FSD 系统的行驶里程突破了 2 万英里（IT之家注：约 32187 公里）。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"居住在美国华盛顿州塔科马市、从事激光雷达（LiDAR）扫描设备销售工作的 Moss，早在 2025 年 12 月便因创下连续 1 万英里使用 FSD v14.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"2、全程零人工接管的纪录而受到关注。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"几天后，他又驾驶车辆从洛杉矶的特斯拉 Diner 出发，一路驶向南卡罗来纳州默特尔比奇，全程 2732 英里（约 4397 公里），耗时两天 20 小时，期间没有发生任何一次人工接管，成为特斯拉历史上首个经验证的横跨美国东西海岸自动驾驶行程。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"微软发布首个网络安全 AI 模型 MAI-Cyber-1-Flash，在 CyberGym 基准测试中取得 95.95% 的成功率，超过 Claude Mythos 5 和 GPT-5.5 Cyber。该模型接入多智能体安全系统 MDASH，可处理约 90% 的网络安全任务，与 GPT-5.4 的组合成本比此前配置降低近 50%。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，微软首席执行官萨蒂亚 · 纳德拉（Satya Nadella）今天（7 月 28 日）在 X 平台发布推文，宣布其首个网络安全模型 MAI-Cyber-1-Flash， 并表示该模型在 CyberGym 基准测试中取得了 95.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"95% 的成功率，超过 Anthropic 和 OpenAI 的同类系统。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"纳德拉表示 MAI-Cyber-1-Flash 是微软首个专门针对网络安全而研发的 AI 模型，并将其接入多智能体（Agent）安全系统 MDASH 中，可以高效处理约 90% 的网络安全任务。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"而针对剩余 10% 较为复杂的网络安全任务，可以调用规模更大、成本更高的模型（官方示例中为 GPT-5.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"成本方面，微软表示，MAI-Cyber-1-Flash 和 GPT-5.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"抖音全面升级未成年人模式适龄推荐算法，核心是引入多模态大语言模型（MLLM）技术，综合处理文本、图像、音频等信息，为每条视频判断适宜观看的年龄范围。平台采用\"人工筛选 + 大模型分类 + 适龄推荐 + 内容偏好匹配\"的多重保障机制，目前未成年人专属内容池总量达770万条，日均新增约6000条优质内容。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，抖音集团今日发布公告称，为了切实缓解家长的监护焦虑，并回应未成年人的成长需求，抖音近期全面升级了未成年人模式的核心引擎 —— 适龄推荐算法。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"当用户开启未成年人模式并设置出生年月后，专属的适龄推荐算法便会随之启动。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"据介绍，本次对未成年人模式推荐算法的全面升级，核心在于引入了前沿的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）技术，构建了一套智能化、精细化的内容识别与分发体系。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"多模态大语言模型具备强大的跨模态语义理解能力，能够综合处理文本、图像、音频等多种信息，并综合考量内容的认知复杂度、价值观导向等，为每一条视频判断其适宜观看的年龄范围，为后续的适龄推荐提供技术基础。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"首先，平台通过未成年人优质多模态大模型对海量信息进行筛选，识别出适宜未成年人观看的候选内容；再由人工专家层层筛选，甄选出兼具正向价值观的优质内容，将其纳入未成年人专属内容池。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]
- evidence_seed: {"company_actions":["IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，抖音集团今日发布公告称，为了切实缓解家长的监护焦虑，并回应未成年人的成长需求，抖音近期全面升级了未成年人模式的核心引擎 —— 适龄推荐算法。","当用户开启未成年人模式并设置出生年月后，专属的适龄推荐算法便会随之启动。","据介绍，本次对未成年人模式推荐算法的全面升级，核心在于引入了前沿的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）技术，构建了一套智能化、精细化的内容识别与分发体系。"],"case_details":[],"workflow_changes":[],"before_after_clues":[],"affected_roles":[],"risks_or_constraints":[]}

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"company_action","text":"美团方面否认 LongCat 团队基础模型负责人裴鹏即将离职的传言。裴鹏于2023年加入美团，主导了万亿参数大模型 LongCat-2.0 的研发与落地。LongCat-2.0 总参数 1.6T，平均激活约 48B，是业界首个在五万卡国产算力集群上完成推理的万亿参数模型，已于7月6日正式开源。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家 7 月 27 日消息，近日，有消息称美团 LongCat 团队基础模型负责人裴鹏即将离职。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"据时代周报向美团方面求证， 对方表示，该消息不属实 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"公开资料显示，裴鹏毕业于北京大学，2023 年加入美团，最初出任计算和智能平台部基座模型团队预训练负责人，后担任 LongCat 团队基础模型负责人，全面牵头美团大语言模型、多模态模型与 Agent 智能体模型的研发工作。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"裴鹏主导 LongCat 系列基础模型研发， 推进万亿参数大模型 LongCat-2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"据IT之家此前报道，6 月 30 日， 美团发布新一代万亿参数大模型 LongCat-2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"case_detail","text":"微软CEO萨提亚·纳德拉警告企业，将AI能力完全交给模型开发商会导致失去竞争力甚至无法生存。他建议企业建立机制，确保每次调用AI模型产生的元数据掌握在自己手中，用于训练自有模型或开源模型，并部署AI Gateway等基础设施隔离Prompt与模型。纳德拉还建议企业不要依赖Claude Code、ChatGPT Codex等内置编程工具，应将上下文和记忆与模型分离，以便自由选择模型并掌握自身命运。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，微软 CEO 萨提亚 · 纳德拉再次向企业发出警告：如果把 AI 能力完全交给 AI 模型开发商，最终可能失去竞争力，甚至无法继续生存。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"当地时间周日（26 日），纳德拉在接受 CNN 采访时表示，企业 不仅要谨慎共享数据，还要谨慎共享 Prompt 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"企业向 AI 模型提供商交出的每一项信息， 都可能成为未来的风险 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"纳德拉认为，企业应建立一套机制，确保每次调用 AI 模型时产生的元数据 都掌握在自己手中 ，以便未来利用这些数据训练自己的模型权重或开源模型。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"模型权重是 AI 模型训练后的核心参数，决定了模型具备怎样的能力。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"company_action","text":"安卓版 Google Translate 在 10.28.62.953449214.8 版本中测试 Live Translate 小部件，用户无需打开应用即可在桌面端点击开始实时翻译。该小部件可自动将听到的语音翻译为目标语言，但无法显示原语言转录内容。目前小部件仅在应用活跃状态下有效，未来版本可能支持离线运行。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，科技媒体 Android Authority 昨日（7 月 27 日）发布博文，报道称 安卓 版谷歌翻译（Google Translate）应用正在测试 Live Translate 小部件， 用户无需打开应用就能翻译相关内容。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"8 版谷歌翻译应用中，谷歌引入 Live Translate 小部件，用户无需打开应用，在桌面端就能通过小部件执行翻译任务。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"界面上，该小部件显示“点击开始实时翻译”提示，以及用户选择的目标翻译语言。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"用户点击该小部件后，立即开始实时翻译。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"页面按钮还支持静音、停止实时翻译和控制扬声器设置。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"Adaptavist 调查显示，约 65% 的白领经常怀念 AI 出现前的工作方式。约三分之一受访者因\"会削弱创造力\"愿放弃生成式 AI，近一半受访者称需花更多时间核实 AI 内容准确性。研究还发现，AI 虽提升创意质量，却削弱了创意多样性，且企业因按 Token 收费而要求员工减少使用 AI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，当地时间 26 日，据英国《金融时报》报道，咨询公司 Adaptavist 调查发现，约 65% 的白领 经常怀念 AI 出现前的工作方式 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"约三分之一的受访者因为“会削弱创造力”而 愿意放弃生成式 AI ；也有约三分之一的人担心 AI 遭到滥用 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"近一半受访者表示，为了核实 AI 生成内容是否准确，自己被迫投入更多时间，结果 与 AI 减少重复劳动的承诺背道而驰 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"一项针对普通人创意写作的研究发现，生成式 AI 虽然能够提供更多剧情转折，却也让 故事越来越相似、创意越来越趋同 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家从报道中获悉，宾夕法尼亚大学沃顿商学院另一项关于创新的研究也得出类似结论：AI 虽然提高了创意质量， 却削弱了创意多样性 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"company_action","text":"Apple 机器学习研究团队提出 GH-ESD（Grounded Hypothesis-Driven Error Slice Discovery），一种针对目标检测与分割等实例级视觉任务的错误切片发现方法。现有方法主要适用于图像级分类，难以捕捉由上下文关系和空间视觉模式导致的实例级失败。GH-ESD 通过基于假设的驱动方式，系统性地发现模型在语义连贯子集上的系统性失效。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Apple 机器学习研究团队提出 GH-ESD（Grounded Hypothesis-Driven Error Slice Discovery），一种针对目标检测与分割等实例级视觉任务的错误切片发现方法。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"现有方法主要适用于图像级分类，难以捕捉由上下文关系和空间视觉模式导致的实例级失败。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"GH-ESD 通过基于假设的驱动方式，系统性地发现模型在语义连贯子集上的系统性失效。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]
